did... But why isn't that kid's face face being shown properly?" Heinkel
pointed out with an annoyed expression.

"Subaru-kun..." Rem held a hand to her chest as she remembered the red

Crusch and Emilia turned away from the Oni as they occupied themselves
with leaning forward as much as they could to see his face again.
Emilia's hand snaked its way to hold Subaru's sleeping hand as tightly
as she always did, being careful not to give her strength more boost, or
else the hand would need to be rehealed again.

*Zooming out away from them, the shot pans to show a grand garden gate

*"If Crusch-sama's goal should be attained, I'm sure it would suit your

"So Russel Fellow-san had his dealin's with ya even prior to me askin'

Crusch's steely look gave the merchant queen some pushback to reassess

"I'm just sayin'... The kids used his ability delightfully to manipulate

"Whatever it is you find yourself complaining about, Anastasia-sama,"

Crusch's lips turn upwards into a small teasing grin. "You've lost five

Anastasia rolled her eyes and gave a small giggle, lightening the mood

"From the looks of that pretty blue maid, I suppose our fella will not

The little tactician of the Hoshin camp made a small hum and nodded his

"Ah hell..." Ricardo sighed tiredly and leaned forward in his seat,

full attention at the very least.

"I mean... It was obvious that this wasn't the one, no?" Mimi shrugged

Her tone was not as excited as it usually is, but that wasn't important

"Ya think Cap'n's gonna die to that thing, Otto-bro?" Garfiel didn't

Otto remained silent and observed with a look of concern etched on his

*The two older men instantly looked to their side to find a black haired

*"My, how unusual it is to see you!" The blonde man commented and smiled

*"My apologies. I am the treasurer of the capital's merchant guilt. My

*The boy said nothing... He didn't reply back. Only watched with Wilhelm

"H-How did he know Subaru's name?" Emilia asked while feeling intensely

"Shouldn't he be mindin' his own business with the Karsten lady?" Felt
finally spoke after a long silence, giving the blonde man on the window

"..." Reinhard looked at the man with scrutinizing eyes.

Ram could feel her master suddenly grow stiff due to the increased
attention on Russel Fellow, for reasons unknown to her of course, but
she had seen many letters being sent back and forth between the Merchant
Guild's true figure and her own master during her hay-day at the old
mansion.

Surely enough, her master began speaking to throw everyone off the scent
she had picked up on years ago.

"He's a powerful politician, Emilia-sam~aaa~. He already had his sights

their Divine Protections of Wind Reading could read through him.

"/Technically I have not lied about Fellow's partial motivation..."/

It was Felt who took his explanation in stride first. "So he seeks to
use Big Bro and scam him? Reinhard, no more deals with that guy."

Reinhard nodded his head, turning from the magi above them to look at
his lady. "I did warn you lady Felt. That man is very shre-"

"No more deals with that guy." Felt repeated without a second to waste
on any accusation that may or may not have been thrown by her knight.

"I understand, Felt-sama." Reinhard realized how serious his lady was as

Emilia just frowned deeply and caressed Subaru's hand. "No one will use

the screen with pure anger. "It's not his fault he was left all alone

Emilia's outrage was met with a small amused snort from the only

"You require a dire lesson in political sociology if you think that boy

The half-elf gripped the unconscious hand harsher than before as she

*"Wilhelm-san, who was that?" Subaru asked with a bored tone as he

*"Russell Fellow. He is, despite what he said, a shrewd man with

*"Now, I was just about to head back inside... "*

*The frozen young man finally turned to face Wilhelm... face still
hidden from view.*

*"Do you have something to say?" The old butler asked while staring at

*Rem glanced at her love with worry in her eyes.*

*Subaru's nasty eyes were never revealed to the screen as it went dark
to light up again.*

/*Opening Theme 2: Paradisus-Paradoxum*/

"Why can't we see Subaru's face, I suppose?" Beatrice asked, a lot more

"I believe it has something to do with what happened in his last loop."
Crusch mused sadly as she remembered the events that led to many in the

"S-Subaru wasn't able to move because of shock in the last one... I

"He tried to act like he couldn't do anything so he could save himself

"Natsuki-san's rage forced him to get a grip I guess," Otto muttered

Garf grimaced and shook his head as he remembered the last face his

Wilhelm looked at his lady as Crusch sent him a glance from the front

"Would you mind entertaining a question I have, Wilhelm-done?" Crusch

"I'm afraid the idea I see in your eye is correct, Crusch-sama," Wilhelm

The duchess grimaced at the confirmation and nodded her head, turning

"This is not at all fair to you..." Crusch muttered and glared down to

Above everyone, Heinkel was glaring at them with increasing confusion.

"I'm tryin' to keep up with these bastards but I can't understand half

"Then perhaps you should get your ears cleaned from that filth,
commoner." Priscilla offered helpfully as she watched the window

"I don't wanna challenge you, Priscilla-sama," Heinkel muttered under

force you and that monster Reinhard in the same room against your will?"

Priscilla huffed and toyed with her fan as she took a moment to glance

Heinkel's eyes actually flared when she insulted Reinhard the way she

"Mineself has been convinced that there's something of interest I must

"I'm here too, Princess..." Al said casually with a wave from the other

Heinkel leaned back in his seat, offering her his silence and inspecting

"Goddamn it..." He muttered, pressing his hand on the armrest.

*The scene changes to show that Subaru had requested an audience with

*Making him sit before her desk with all of Felix, Wilhelm, and Rem

*"Some people calling themselves the Witch's Cult plan to attack

"The Witch's Cult?!" Schult had a concerned expression immediately,

"He's dealing with the attack on the L. Mathers domain... The one where

"Th~aaa~t's the one, dear." Roswaal held a small smile for the clueless

Schult locked eyes with Heinkel for a moment. "Heinkel-sama... We're

Heinkel's eyes immediately turned to his father and son below them, his

Wilhelm and Reinhard didn't look at their family member, remaining

"Could that mean that Subaru already made the deal with Crusch-sama and

"I'm afraid I'll have to dispel that notion..." Crusch brought a hand to

"You won't help him..." Emilia finalized Crusch's thought with a

Everyone turned to Crusch with wide eyes. Felix's hopeful look turned to

The lady was looking down at her knees with a guilt-ridden face.

"What do ya mean?" Garfiel asked in alarm.

Crusch seemed to crumble in on herself as she looked away from Emilia's

"He... He still hadn't made contact with the Whale..." Crusch's steeled

Beatrice and Rem stared at the lady with alarmed faces. "Then... How

Emilia looked just as frightened as the spirit, yet Rem from behind both

"That's why he wanted to run away... After so many things going

*"I see. So they have made their move." Crusch commented with a frown.*

*"It seems so. Of course, from the moyment Emilia-sama, a half-elf,

Emilia winced and tried to hide one of her ears with the hand she was

*"I understand the situation. My next question is why you chose me to

*"Because, to be honest, you seem the most likely to help right now.

"He's not using his head," Otto muttered under his breath, using his

*"Don't misunderstand. I helped you two because I was contracted to do

"Don't worry about that shit, kid," Heinkel grunted, patting the kid's

"I hope so..." Schult muttered as he looked up at the window again.

*He remained silent for a split second. "If you help me out with this

*"Should I accept that proposal, it would mean Emilia drops out of the

*Subaru's face wasn't completely shown, but his mouth was shown forming

"He has nothing to give right now... So if he was refused in here..."

"That is why he used the white whale in his deal with us... he learned

"Crusch-sama..." Emilia looked a little betrayed when she faced the

"I... Have no words to justify myself. It's a rivalry for the throne and

"..." Emilia couldn't look at the woman either.

*"What did you expect? To place your own domain's survival in the hands

"Why can't this wretched glass panel show the commoner's face?"

Emilia clenched her fists tightly as the words echoed in her head. They

"Maybe... I'm not ready to be King... Who would bring such pain and

*Subaru's teeth grit in nervousness but his eyes remained obscure to the

"He agreed?!" Otto shouted in surprise, bringing Julius and Garfiel to

"Subaru..." Emilia looked down and nodded her head. It was his best

Emilia rubbed his hand and looked down at the sleeping face of her

"Goddamn, this guy just gave up his lady like she was nothin'..."

*The green-haired matriarch let her stern expression fall for a moment

*"Huh?" Subaru's mouth is shown to be slackened. He hangs back in his

*"First of all, the condition of Emilia's withdrawal, which you offered

*"Are you really not aware? Emilia's elimination from the royal

"Watch what you say!" Felix glared at the merchant, growing discontent

Petra brought a hand to her mouth, unable to stop her respect for the

Wilhelm looked at Frederica with understanding eyes in reply to her

Petra could only look away from the old man with an angry pout on her

*Felix growled and stepped forward with a glare. "Hey! I've heard enough

*Crusch raised a hand. "It's fine," she keeps her eyes on Subaru's which

*"That's bull-" Subaru's teeth gritted in a nasty manner, and his eyes

Garfiel looked frustrated like many in the crowd around him. "Damn, she

"Subaru Natuski can't tell her how he obtained such information so

Crusch braved the awful looks being sent her way and steeled herself to

Emilia looked at the duchess with a thin-lipped expression that showed

"Don't take this on alone, my lady..." Felix winced as he reached for

*"Crusch-sama, please don't make jokes. Of course, Subaru-kun isn't a

*"Really? Given what he said, if he can't explain how he knows these

"I hope this other me can help him..." Rem held on to some hope in her

"You wish for nothing but pain to fill his heart..." Ram's words went by

"I don't want him to be in pain... I just... I just want to be able to

*Crusch stared at the maid for a moment and looked down. "Regardless, I

*"The Witch's Cult is coming!" Subaru shouted, making all in the room

*"They're going to slaughter everyone in the village!" Subaru growled in

"That's not... The way to go about this..." Heinkel looked almost in

"Hold on..." Otto growled under his breath. "This isn't like him..."

"You're right Otto-bro," Garfiel nodded his head with a small amount of

"Roswaal-sama... may you remove that intrigued look in your eyes,
